# Production governance table audit

Source commit: `1ef4b60aa8c74b57a9d1096f5b0d7711ef800353`. PostgreSQL snapshot: `211413:211413:`. Audited at: 2026-10-19T12:55:43.926Z.

**335 actual tables; 490,765 source rows; 1 import-manifest rows.**

Executed table checks complete: True. Explicitly unsupported checks below remain unresolved.

This is a read-only audit. No records were deleted, changed or approved for deletion. Counts and content fingerprints are from production, not the removed SQLite file.

## How to interpret the findings

- Exact duplicates compare every source column and exclude only the technical import ordinal.
- Non-key payload repetition omits source primary keys; repeated history, version or matrix records may be intentional. It is not a safe deletion count.
- Retirement markers are explicit retired/obsolete/deprecated/superseded/deleted/archived values. Inactive is reported separately and is not treated as obsolete.
- Declared-key checks use source metadata. Unsupported affinity, collation, expression and partial-index semantics remain unresolved.
- Static mentions are evidence for review, not proof of runtime use or disuse. No-reference tables are not automatically obsolete.
- All deletions remain blocked pending record identity/replacement review, undeclared JSON/external references, ownership and evidence-retention requirements.

## Scope and validity

- No records were exported, deleted, changed or reclassified in the database.
- Duplicates and retirement markers are review candidates, not approved deletions. Repeated evidence can be intentional.
- Source primary/unique/foreign key declarations are metadata; they were not all recreated as PostgreSQL constraints.
- Foreign-key checks with unresolved source affinity, collation or partial/expression semantics are explicitly not checked.
- Static source mentions do not prove runtime use, and no mentions do not prove obsolescence. JSON/external references remain unresolved.
- Date prefixes are observations, not retention eligibility. No retention policy is invented.
- This audit is bound to the database snapshot/content fingerprints and repository source; rerun after either changes.
